* *Description:* Changes the panning of the currently playing music.
* *Parameters:*
** `+panning+` (number): The new panning value.
* *Return Value:* None.

'''''

==== music.setRate()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
music.setRate(rate);
----

* *Description:* Changes the playback rate of the currently playing music.
* *Parameters:*
** `+rate+` (number): The new playback rate.
* *Return Value:* None.

'''''

==== music.isPlaying()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let playing = music.isPlaying();
----

* *Description:* Checks if the music is currently playing.
* *Parameters:* None.
* *Return Value:* 1 if playing, 0 otherwise.

'''''

==== music.pause()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
music.pause();
----

* *Description:* Pauses the currently playing music without resetting the playback position.
* *Parameters:* None.
* *Return Value:* The music object (for chaining), or null if invalid.

'''''

==== music.resume()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
music.resume();
----

* *Description:* Resumes a paused music track from where it was paused.
* *Parameters:* None.
* *Return Value:* The music object (for chaining), or null if invalid.

'''''

==== music.isPaused()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let paused = music.isPaused();
----

* *Description:* Checks if the music is currently paused.
* *Parameters:* None.
* *Return Value:* 1 if paused, 0 otherwise.

'''''

=== Example: Audio Usage

[source,js]
----
// Create and play sound effects
let explosionSound = SoundManager.create("explosion", "sounds/explosion.wav", 4, 0);
let jumpSound = SoundManager.create("jump", "sounds/jump.wav", 2, 0);

// Play sound effects
explosionSound.play(1.0, 0.0, 1.0);

// Play with 3D spatialization
let enemyPos = Vector3.create(10, 0, 5);
let enemyVel = Vector3.create(0, 0, 0);
let voiceID = explosionSound.playSpatialization(0.8, 0.0, 1.0, 1, enemyPos, enemyVel, 0);

// Create and play background music
let bgMusic = MusicManager.create("bgm", "music/background.ogg");
bgMusic.play(0.6, 0.0, 1.0, 1); // Loop the music

// Later, fade out music by reducing volume
bgMusic.setVolume(0.3);

// Pause and resume music
bgMusic.pause();
// ... some time later ...
bgMusic.resume();

// Stop music
bgMusic.stop();

// Clean up
SoundManager.clear();
MusicManager.clear();
----

'''''

== Scene3D

=== Overview

The Scene3D API exposes the PasVulkan 3D scene-graph to POCA scripts.  It is
accessed through the `+engine.scene3d+` object.

NOTE: `+engine.scene3d+` is *not* created by the engine itself — it is
injected by the *game application* at context initialisation time by calling
`+InitializePOCAScene3DContext(context, scene3DInstance, engineHash)+`
(implemented in `+PasVulkan.POCA.Scene3D.pas+`).  In a typical game project
this call is made in the game's own POCA setup unit.  A game that does not call
`+InitializePOCAScene3DContext+` will not have an `+engine.scene3d+` object at
all.

All objects returned by the Scene3D API are *ghost objects*.  A ghost holds
a *pointer* to the underlying engine resource but does **not** hold an
independent reference-count on it.  The resource is owned by the scene.

When the POCA garbage collector finalises a ghost, only the internal pointer
is nulled — the engine resource itself is **not** released.  Call `.destroy()`
on a ghost to explicitly release (DecRef) the resource immediately and null
the pointer.  Any resource not explicitly destroyed is cleaned up by
`+TpvScene3D.Destroy+` at scene / game shutdown.

*Do not* call `+.destroy()+` on ghosts that you still intend to use.  Holding
a ghost variable alive keeps the pointer valid, but the resource stays alive
regardless (it is owned by the scene).

'''''

=== engine.scene3d — Scene object

`+engine.scene3d+` is a ghost object injected by the game application (see
Overview above).  It is the entry point for all Scene3D operations.  All
factory methods on it return ghost objects that the caller is responsible for
keeping alive (or explicitly destroying).

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.createImage(width, height, pixels)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let img = engine.scene3d.createImage(width, height, pixels);
----

* *Description:* Creates a `+Scene3DImage+` ghost from an array of packed RGBA8
  pixel values.  Pixels are encoded as `+0xRRGGBBAA+` 32-bit integers stored in
  the `+pixels+` array in row-major order.  The underlying image data is stored
  as QOI internally.
* *Parameters:*
** `+width+` (number): Image width in pixels.
** `+height+` (number): Image height in pixels.
** `+pixels+` (array): Array of `+width * height+` 32-bit RGBA values.
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DImage+` ghost, or `+null+` on failure.
* *Notes:* The image is not yet uploaded to the GPU after creation.  Call
  `+img.upload()+` once per render frame until `+img.isUploaded()+` returns
  `+true+`, or simply upload before first use.
* *Example:*
+
[source,js]
----
// 2×2 white image
let pixels = [0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFFFFFFF];
let img = engine.scene3d.createImage(2, 2, pixels);
----

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.loadImage(path)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let img = engine.scene3d.loadImage("textures/rock.png");
----

* *Description:* Loads an image from the application asset bundle by virtual
  path.  Supported formats are those understood by the engine (PNG, JPEG, QOI,
  KTX2, …).  The image is fully loaded and uploaded synchronously during this
  call.
* *Parameters:*
** `+path+` (string): Asset-relative virtual path.
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DImage+` ghost, or `+null+` if the asset does not
  exist or loading fails.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.getImageByName(name)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let img = engine.scene3d.getImageByName("my_image");
----

* *Description:* Looks up an already-loaded image by its internal name.
* *Parameters:*
** `+name+` (string): The image name (set during creation or loading).
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DImage+` ghost (new owned reference), or `+null+` if
  no image with that name exists.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.createSampler([type])

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let s = engine.scene3d.createSampler();            // default repeat
let s = engine.scene3d.createSampler('nonrepeat');
let s = engine.scene3d.createSampler('mipmap');
let s = engine.scene3d.createSampler('mipmapnonrepeat');
----

* *Description:* Creates a new `+Scene3DSampler+` ghost with the given filter
  and wrap preset.
* *Parameters:*
** `+type+` (string, optional): One of `+'default'+`, `+'nonrepeat'+`,
   `+'mipmap'+`, `+'mipmapnonrepeat'+`.  Defaults to `+'default'+` (bilinear,
   repeat).
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DSampler+` ghost.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.defaultSampler()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let s = engine.scene3d.defaultSampler();
----

* *Description:* Returns the scene's built-in default sampler (bilinear, repeat
  wrapping) as an owned ghost reference.
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DSampler+` ghost, or `+null+` if no default sampler
  exists.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.defaultNonRepeatSampler()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let s = engine.scene3d.defaultNonRepeatSampler();
----

* *Description:* Returns the scene's built-in default non-repeat sampler
  (bilinear, clamp-to-edge) as an owned ghost reference.
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DSampler+` ghost, or `+null+` if not available.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.createTexture(image [, sampler])

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let tex = engine.scene3d.createTexture(img);
let tex = engine.scene3d.createTexture(img, sampler);
----

* *Description:* Creates a `+Scene3DTexture+` ghost by combining a
  `+Scene3DImage+` and an optional `+Scene3DSampler+`.  When no sampler is
  supplied the scene's default repeat sampler is used automatically.
* *Parameters:*
** `+image+` (`+Scene3DImage+`): The image ghost to attach.
** `+sampler+` (`+Scene3DSampler+`, optional): A sampler ghost.  Defaults to
   the scene default.
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DTexture+` ghost, or `+null+` on failure.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.createTextureFromImage(width, height, pixels)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let tex = engine.scene3d.createTextureFromImage(width, height, pixels);
----

* *Description:* Convenience shortcut that creates an image from a pixel array
  and immediately wraps it in a texture using the default sampler.  No separate
  image ghost is returned; the image is owned by the texture.
* *Parameters:*
** `+width+` (number): Image width in pixels.
** `+height+` (number): Image height in pixels.
** `+pixels+` (array): Array of `+width * height+` packed RGBA8 values
   (`+0xRRGGBBAA+`).
* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DTexture+` ghost, or `+null+` on failure.

'''''

==== engine.scene3d.createMaterial([opts])

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
let mat = engine.scene3d.createMaterial();
let mat = engine.scene3d.createMaterial({
  name: 'myMaterial',
  shadingModel: 'pbr',          // 'pbr' | 'specularGlossiness' | 'unlit'
  alphaMode:    'opaque',       // 'opaque' | 'mask' | 'blend'
  alphaCutoff:  0.5,
  doubleSided:  false,
  castShadows:  true,
  receiveShadows: true,
  noWetness:    false,
  baseColor:    [1, 1, 1, 1],  // [r, g, b, a]
  metallic:     0.0,
  roughness:    0.5,
  emissive:     [0, 0, 0],     // [r, g, b]
  baseColorTexture:          tex,
  normalTexture:             normalTex,
  metallicRoughnessTexture:  mrTex,
  occlusionTexture:          aoTex,
  emissiveTexture:           emTex,
  hologram: {
    active:        false,
    direction:     [0, 1, 0],
    flickerSpeed:  1.0,
    flickerMin:    0.5,
    flickerMax:    1.0,
    mainColor:     [0, 0.8, 1, 1],
    rimColor:      [0, 0.5, 1, 1],
    rimPower:      2.0,
    rimThreshold:  0.2,
    scanTiling:    1.0,
    scanSpeed:     0.5,
    scanMin:       0.0,
    scanMax:       1.0,
    glowTiling:    1.0,
    glowSpeed:     0.3,
    glowMin:       0.0,
    glowMax:       1.0,
  },
});
----

* *Description:* Creates a new `+Scene3DMaterial+` ghost.  All fields in the
  optional `+opts+` hash are applied atomically before `+FillShaderData+` is
  called, so no intermediate GPU updates are generated.
* *Parameters:*
** `+opts+` (hash, optional): Initial property values.  All keys are optional
   and use the defaults of the engine material if omitted.

[cols="1,1,3", options="header"]
|===
|Key |Type |Description

|`+name+` |string |Internal material name (default: `+'poca_material'+`).
|`+shadingModel+` |string |`+'pbr'+` (default), `+'specularGlossiness'+`, or `+'unlit'+`.
|`+alphaMode+` |string |`+'opaque'+` (default), `+'mask'+`, or `+'blend'+`.
|`+alphaCutoff+` |number |Alpha cutoff threshold for `+'mask'+` mode (default 0.5).
|`+doubleSided+` |bool |Disable back-face culling (default `+false+`).
|`+castShadows+` |bool |Whether the material casts shadows (default `+true+`).
|`+receiveShadows+` |bool |Whether the material receives shadows (default `+true+`).
|`+noWetness+` |bool |Suppress wetness/rain interaction (default `+false+`).
|`+baseColor+` |array[4] |Base colour factor `+[r, g, b, a]+` (default `+[1,1,1,1]+`).
|`+metallic+` |number |Metallic factor 0–1 (default 0).
|`+roughness+` |number |Roughness factor 0–1 (default 1).
|`+emissive+` |array[3] |Emissive colour factor `+[r, g, b]+` (default `+[0,0,0]+`).
|`+baseColorTexture+` |Scene3DTexture |Base colour / albedo texture.
|`+normalTexture+` |Scene3DTexture |Tangent-space normal map.
|`+metallicRoughnessTexture+` |Scene3DTexture |Metallic-roughness texture (B=metallic, G=roughness).
|`+occlusionTexture+` |Scene3DTexture |Ambient-occlusion texture (R channel).
|`+emissiveTexture+` |Scene3DTexture |Emissive colour texture.
|`+hologram+` |hash |Hologram sub-object (see hologram table below).
|===

[cols="1,1,3", options="header"]
|===
|Hologram key |Type |Description

|`+active+` |bool |Enable hologram rendering (default `+false+`).
|`+direction+` |array[3] |Scan/glow direction vector `+[x, y, z]+`.
|`+flickerSpeed+` |number |Flicker animation speed.
|`+flickerMin+` |number |Minimum flicker intensity.
|`+flickerMax+` |number |Maximum flicker intensity.
|`+mainColor+` |array[4] |Main hologram colour `+[r, g, b, a]+`.
|`+rimColor+` |array[4] |Rim light colour `+[r, g, b, a]+`.
|`+rimPower+` |number |Fresnel rim exponent.
|`+rimThreshold+` |number |Fresnel rim threshold.
|`+scanTiling+` |number |Scan-line tiling.
|`+scanSpeed+` |number |Scan-line scroll speed.
|`+scanMin+` |number |Scan-line intensity minimum.
|`+scanMax+` |number |Scan-line intensity maximum.
|`+glowTiling+` |number |Glow tiling.
|`+glowSpeed+` |number |Glow scroll speed.
|`+glowMin+` |number |Glow intensity minimum.
|`+glowMax+` |number |Glow intensity maximum.
|===

* *Return Value:* A `+Scene3DMaterial+` ghost.

'''''

=== Scene3DImage

A ghost object wrapping a `+TpvScene3D.TImage+`.  The ghost holds a pointer
to the image; the image itself is owned by the scene.  GC-finalised ghosts
only null their pointer.  Call `+img.destroy()+` to release (DecRef) explicitly.

'''''

==== img.upload()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
img.upload();
----

* *Description:* Triggers a GPU upload for the image (synchronous staging
  enqueue).  Due to the render-frame pipeline, the upload may not be visible
  until the next rendered frame.  Call repeatedly until `+img.isUploaded()+`
  returns `+true+` if synchronous availability is required.
* *Return Value:* The image ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== img.isUploaded()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
if (img.isUploaded()) { ... }
----

* *Description:* Returns `+true+` (`+1+`) if the GPU upload has completed, or
  `+false+` (`+0+`) if it is still pending.
* *Return Value:* `+1+` or `+0+`.

'''''

==== img.destroy()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
img.destroy();
----

* *Description:* Immediately releases the ghost's reference on the image.  The
  ghost's internal pointer is nulled; any further method calls on the same
  variable are silently ignored.
* *Return Value:* `+null+`.

'''''

=== Scene3DSampler

A ghost object wrapping a `+TpvScene3D.TSampler+`.  The ghost holds a
pointer; the sampler is owned by the scene.  GC-finalised ghosts only null
their pointer.  Call `+sampler.destroy()+` to release explicitly.

'''''

==== sampler.destroy()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
sampler.destroy();
----

* *Description:* Immediately releases the ghost's reference on the sampler.
* *Return Value:* `+null+`.

'''''

=== Scene3DTexture

A ghost object wrapping a `+TpvScene3D.TTexture+`.  The ghost holds a
pointer; the texture is owned by the scene.  GC-finalised ghosts only null
their pointer.  Call `+tex.destroy()+` to release explicitly.

'''''

==== tex.destroy()

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
tex.destroy();
----

* *Description:* Immediately releases the ghost's reference on the texture.
* *Return Value:* `+null+`.

'''''

=== Scene3DMaterial

A ghost object wrapping a `+TpvScene3D.TMaterial+`.  After any setter call the
material automatically calls `+FillShaderData+` and notifies the scene of a new
material data generation so GPU buffers are updated.  Use `+fillShaderData()+`
explicitly to batch multiple setters and trigger only one GPU update.

'''''

==== mat.setShadingModel(model)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setShadingModel('pbr');                // PBR metallic-roughness (default)
mat.setShadingModel('specularGlossiness'); // PBR specular-glossiness
mat.setShadingModel('unlit');              // Unlit / no lighting
----

* *Parameters:*
** `+model+` (string): `+'pbr'+`, `+'specularGlossiness'+`, or `+'unlit'+`.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setAlphaMode(mode)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setAlphaMode('opaque'); // (default)
mat.setAlphaMode('mask');
mat.setAlphaMode('blend');
----

* *Parameters:*
** `+mode+` (string): `+'opaque'+`, `+'mask'+`, or `+'blend'+`.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setAlphaCutoff(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setAlphaCutoff(0.5);
----

* *Description:* Sets the alpha cutoff threshold used when `+alphaMode+` is
  `+'mask'+`.
* *Parameters:*
** `+value+` (number): Alpha cutoff 0–1.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setDoubleSided(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setDoubleSided(true);
----

* *Description:* Enables or disables back-face culling.
* *Parameters:*
** `+value+` (bool/number): `+true+`/`+1+` = double-sided (no culling).
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setCastShadows(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]

----

* *Parameters:*
** `+value+` (bool/number): `+true+`/`+1+` = casts shadows (default).
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setReceiveShadows(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----

----

==== mat.setNoWetness(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]

  effects.
* *Parameters:*

==== mat.setBaseColorFactor(r, g, b, a)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setBaseColorFactor(1.0, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);         // scalar form
mat.setBaseColorFactor([1.0, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0]);        // array form

----

* *Description:* Sets the base colour (albedo) factor.
* *Parameters (Option A):*
** `+r+`, `+g+`, `+b+`, `+a+` (number): RGBA components, each 0–1.
* *Parameters (Option B):*
** `+color+` (Vector4, array[4], or hash `{r,g,b,a}`): RGBA colour. See <<Vector Argument Conventions>>.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setMetallicFactor(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setMetallicFactor(1.0);
----

* *Parameters:*
** `+value+` (number): Metallic factor 0–1.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setRoughnessFactor(value)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setRoughnessFactor(0.3);
----

* *Parameters:*
** `+value+` (number): Roughness factor 0–1.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setEmissiveFactor(r, g, b)

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setEmissiveFactor(1.0, 0.5, 0.0);            // scalar form
mat.setEmissiveFactor([1.0, 0.5, 0.0]);           // array form
mat.setEmissiveFactor({r: 1.0, g: 0.5, b: 0.0}); // hash form
mat.setEmissiveFactor(Vector3.create(1.0, 0.5, 0.0)); // ghost form
----

* *Description:* Sets the emissive colour factor.  The emissive strength
  component (w) is not modified.
* *Parameters (Option A):*
** `+r+`, `+g+`, `+b+` (number): RGB components, each 0–1 (or higher for HDR).
* *Parameters (Option B):*
** `+color+` (Vector3, array[3], or hash `{r,g,b}`): RGB colour. See <<Vector Argument Conventions>>.
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setBaseColorTexture(tex [, texCoord])

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setBaseColorTexture(tex);
mat.setBaseColorTexture(tex, 1); // use UV set 1
----

* *Description:* Assigns the base colour / albedo texture.
* *Parameters:*
** `+tex+` (`+Scene3DTexture+`): The texture ghost.
** `+texCoord+` (number, optional): UV channel index (default `+0+`).
* *Return Value:* The material ghost (for method chaining).

'''''

==== mat.setNormalTexture(tex [, scale [, texCoord]])

*Usage:*

[source,js]
----
mat.setNormalTexture(normalTex);
mat.setNormalTexture(normalTex, 2.0);     // double normal strength
mat.setNormalTexture(normalTex, 1.0, 1);  // UV set 1
----

* *Description:* Assigns the tangent-space normal map.
* *Parameters:*
** `+tex+` (`+Scene3DTexture+`): The normal map texture.
** `+scale+` (number, optional): Normal map scale/strength (default `+1.0+`).
** `+texCoord+` (number, optional): UV channel inde
